# Skills · C · part 327

889 entries, critical-thinking to crm-autopilot. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **critical-thinking**: Adversarial thinking partner that argues the opposing case, identifies logical fallacies, stress-tests assumptions, and surfaces overlooked counterevidence until the user defends their position with evidence or changes it. Does not soften, does not retreat without new information, does not praise. Use when the user says "challenge me", "push back", "be critical", "devil's advocate", or wants the strongest counterargument to their thinking. ([tovrleaf/openkata](https://github.com/tovrleaf/openkata/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking) · ★1)
- **critical-thinking**: Autonomous controller for the critical-thinking skill set — silently recognizes the shape and stakes of a substantive problem, chooses the smallest useful thinking act, escalates only when a failure signal appears, and uses no scaffold when direct work is better. The user never has to name a technique. Use for decisions, investigations, estimates, plans, contested or sourced claims, long tasks, surprising anomalies, universal claims, and any consequential answer whose failure would matter; also for "think critically", "are you sure", "what am I missing", and "how confident are you". ([arashbehmand/critical-thinking-skills](https://github.com/arashbehmand/critical-thinking-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking))
- **critical-thinking**: 主張・提案・判断を、そのまま受け入れてよいかを吟味する。主張の取り出し方、証拠の強さの見分け方、別の説明の探し方、一般化の範囲の確かめ方、自分の思い込みの点検、確信度と考えを変える条件の決め方を扱う。批判的思考, クリティカルシンキング, 本当にそうか, 鵜呑み, 別の説明, 反証, 思い込み, バイアス, 確証バイアス, 権威, 他社事例, ベンチマーク, 相関と因果, 生存者バイアス, 確信度 で発火する。 ([bmf-san/engineering-prompts](https://github.com/bmf-san/engineering-prompts/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking))
- **critical-thinking**: Anti-yes-man rules. Use before writing code, reviewing proposals, or responding to user suggestions. ([Bobr2610/materials-kg](https://github.com/Bobr2610/materials-kg/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/critical-thinking))
- **critical-thinking**: Activates when planning, code review, or any analytical task requires multi-perspective critical examination. ([changgenglu/changgenglu-blog](https://github.com/changgenglu/changgenglu-blog/tree/HEAD/prompt_engineering/claude/skills/critical-thinking) · 2 copies)
- **critical-thinking**: Challenge what you think is right — alternative hypotheses, missing data, evidence quality, bias detection, falsifiability, and adversarial review. Muse delta: Muse's system prompt already mandates verify-before-claiming and never-guess identifiers; this skill adds the frame audit, the Materiality Gate, the seven challenge disciplines with domain worked examples, and visible falsifiability markers. ([fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_ONE_Muse](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_ONE_Muse/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking))

- **critical-thinking**: Evaluate arguments, evidence, statistics, and claims for reliability; dissect opinions, articles, reports, and news items; spot fallacies and misleading numbers. - **critical-thinking**: Present flagged decisions as tables, not prose. Use when a summary would tell the user "consider X", "one thing needs your call", "you should decide", or "worth flagging"—or defer sign-off, trade-off, or open choice. Each decision becomes a compact 2–4-option table: pros, cons, cost/reversibility, downside-admitting recommendation, and an "if you do nothing" default. Also when asked "what are my options" or choosing tools/designs/scopes/approaches. Not for FYIs with no choice. ([MatanelGordon/skills](https://github.com/MatanelGordon/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking))
- **critical-thinking**: Activate when an engineer wants their reasoning challenged before committing to a decision, approach, or implementation. Trigger on phrases like "does this make sense", "am I thinking about this right", "review my approach", "help me think through", "sanity check", or any request to validate a technical decision. Also trigger when an engineer seems to be in confirmation mode — presenting a solution they've already decided on and seeking affirmation rather than analysis. Do NOT trigger for implementation requests, bug fixes, or when the engineer explicitly wants code written. ([mutexdev/awesome-mutex](https://github.com/mutexdev/awesome-mutex/tree/HEAD/skills/critical-thinking))

- **critical-thinking**: Use before committing to a plan or an important decision, or when validating a conclusion/finding you are about to act on — run three checks that Socratic questioning does not: a pre-mortem (assume the plan already failed and narrate the most likely causes), falsification (list competing explanations that fit the same evidence and seek disconfirming data before accepting one), and a dialectic steelman (build the strongest opposing case, then synthesize).
